vṛtterabhinnarūpatvātteṣu vṛttirna vidyate  || 3,14.137 ||
rūpācca śabdasaṃskāraḥ sāmānyaviṣayo yataḥ  |
tasmāttadāśrayaṃ liṅgaṃ vacanaṃ ca prasajyate  || 3,14.138 ||
saliṅgaṃ ca sasaṃkhyaṃ ca tato dravyābhidhāyinā  |
saṃbadhyate padaṃ tatra tayorbhinnā śrutirbhavet || 3,14.139 ||
bhāvino bahiraṅgasya vacanādāśrayasya ye  |
liṅgasaṃkhye guṇānāṃ te sūtreṇa pratipādite  || 3,14.140 ||
viśeṣavṛtterapi ca rūpābhedādalakṣitaḥ  |
yasmādviśeṣastenātra bhedakāryaṃ na kalpate  || 3,14.141 ||
viśeṣa eva sāmānyaṃ viśesādbhidyate yataḥ  |
abhedo hi viśeṣāṇāmāśrito vinivartakaḥ  || 3,14.142 ||
yadyadāśrīyate tattadanyasya vinivartakam  |
bhedābhedavibhāgastu sāmānye na nirūpyate  || 3,14.143 ||
apoddhāraśca sāmānyamiti tasyopakārinaḥ  |
nimittāvasthamevātastatsvadharmeṇa gṛhyate  || 3,14.144 ||
anirdhāritadharmatvādbhedā eva vikalpitāḥ  |
nimittairvyapadiśyante sāmānyākhyāviśesitāḥ  || 3,14.145 ||
yadā tu vyapadiśyete liṅgasaṃkhye svabhāvataḥ  |
prayogeṣveva sādhutvaṃ vākye prakramyate tadā  || 3,14.146 ||
tatra prayogo'niyato guṇānāmāśrayaiḥ saha  |
sāmānyaṃ yattadatyantaṃ tatraiva samavasthitam  || 3,14.147 ||
na gotvaṃ śābaleyasya gauriti vyapadiśyate  |
śuklatvaṃ bāhuleyasya śukla ityapadiśyate  || 3,14.148 ||
vyatireke ca satyevaṃ matupaḥ śravanaṃ bhavet |
luganvākhyāyate tasmādrasādibhyaśca nāsti saḥ  || 3,14.149 ||
yatso'yamiti saṃbandhādrūpābhedena vartate  |
śuklādivattato lopastadrasādau na vidyate  || 3,14.150 ||
āveśo liṅgasaṃkhyābhyāṃ kva cinmañcādivatsthitah |
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhe sa prasthādau na vidyate  || 3,14.151 ||
liṅgaṃ liṅgaparityāge sūtraṃ pratyayaśāsanam  |
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhātpuṃśabde stryabhidhāyini  || 3,14.152 ||
āśraye liṅgasaṃkhyābhyāmāśritaṃ vyapadiśyate  |
viśeṣaṇānāṃ cājāteriti śāstravyavasthayā  || 3,14.153 ||
nimittānuvidhāyitvādye dharmā bhedahetuṣu  |
ta āśraye'pi vidyanta iti buddhirnivartyate  || 3,14.154 ||
ākhyāyate ca śāstreṇa lokarūḍhā svabhāvataḥ  |
nimittatulyā godādau pravṛttirliṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ  || 3,14.155 ||
haritakyādiṣu vyaktiḥ saṃkhyā khalatikādiṣu  |
manuṣyalubviśeṣāṇāmabhidheyāśrayaṃ dvayam  || 3,14.156 ||
jātiprayoge jātyā cetsaṃbandhamupagacchati  |
viśeṣaṇaṃ tato dharmāñjātestatpratipadyate  || 3,14.157 ||
lubante saṃnipatitaṃ jāteranyadviśeṣaṇam  |
lubantasya pradhānatvāttaddharmairvyapadiśyate  || 3,14.158 ||
nañsamāsabahuvrīhi- dvandvastryatiśayeṣu ye  |
bhedā bhāṣyānusāreṇa vācyāste liṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ  || 3,14.159 ||
yadi ṣaṣṭhīdvitīyāntānnikṛṣṭāttamabādayaḥ  |
nyakkāriṇi syurutkṛṣṭe prakṛteḥ syādviliṅgatā  || 3,14.160 ||
kālyāṃ kālāddvitīyāntātkāle kālyāstarabbhavet |
nyakkāriṇi tathā gārgye gargebhyaḥ pratyayo bhavet || 3,14.161 ||
nyakkartṛṣu ca gargeṣu gārgyātsyāttacca neṣyate  |
kumāryāḥ svārthike ṅīpsyātprakṛtyartho hi nādhikaḥ  || 3,14.162 ||
ṣaṣṭhyantādadhike tasmādguṇe svāśrayavartini  |
utkṛṣṭasamavetāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ vā vidhīyate  || 3,14.163 ||
upāttaṃ ca prakṛtyartho dravyamevāśrayastayoḥ  |
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhādabhedena pratīyate  || 3,14.164 ||
rūpābhedācca taddravyamākāṅkṣāvatpratīyate  |
viśeṣairbhinnarūpaistadāśrayairiva yujyate  || 3,14.165 ||
bhinnarūpesu yalliṅgaṃ viśeṣesu vyavasthitam  |
saṃkhyā ca tābhyāṃ dravyātmā so'bhinno vyapadiśyate  || 3,14.166 ||
āśrayaḥ samavāyi ca nimittaṃ liṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ  |
kartṛsthabhāvakaḥ śetirato bhāṣya udāhṛtaḥ  || 3,14.167 ||
nimittamāśrayatvena gṛhyeta yadi sādhanam  |
karmāpadiṣṭayoḥ prāptistatra syālliṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ  || 3,14.168 ||
śāstre nimittabhāvena samudāyādapoddhṛtaḥ  |
stryarthastasyecchayā yogaḥ prakṛtyā pratyayena vā  || 3,14.169 ||
strīśabdo guṇaśabdatvāttulyadharmā sitādibhiḥ  |
guṇamātre prayujyeta saṃstyānavati vāśraye  || 3,14.170 ||
stryarthaḥ saṃstyānavaddravyaṃ prakṛtyarthaśca yadyasau  |
dravyopalakṣaṇārthatvaṃ saṃstyānasya tathā sati  || 3,14.171 ||
saṃstyānena kva ciddravyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ yadyupalakṣitam  |
anaṅgīkṛtasaṃstyānāttadvṛtteḥ pratyayo bhavet || 3,14.172 ||
bhūtādayaḥ ṣaḍākhyāśca saṃstyānenopalakṣite  |
brāhmaṇyādau yadā vṛttāstebhyaḥ syuḥ pratyayāstadā  || 3,14.173 ||
tadvanto hi pradhānatvātpratyayāṇāṃ prayojakāḥ  |
sāmānādhikaraiṇye'pi tasmāṭṭābādisaṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.174 ||
guṇamātrābhidhāyitvaṃ strīśabde varṇyate yadā  |
prakṛtyarthaśca saṃstyānaṃ svārthikāḥ pratyayāstadā  || 3,14.175 ||
saṃstyāne kevale vṛttiḥ prakṛtīnāṃ na vidyate  |
tadāviṣṭe tato dravye gṛhyante samavasthitāḥ  || 3,14.176 ||
upakāri ca saṃstyānaṃ yeṣu śabdeṣvapekṣitam  |
tebhyaṣṭābādayastacca bhūtādiṣvavivakṣitam  || 3,14.177 ||
saṃstyānaṃ pratyayasyārthaḥ śuddhamāśrīyate yadā  |
tadā dvivacanāneka- pratyayatvaṃ na sidhyati  || 3,14.178 ||
jātiścetstrītvamevāsau bhedo'nyatrāvivakṣitaḥ  |
yasmādbhinnairapi dravyaistadekaṃ sadviśiṣyate  || 3,14.179 ||
mātrāṇāṃ hi tirobhāve parimāṇaṃ na vidyate  |
kumārya iti tena syātkumāryāṃ bhedasaṃbhavāt || 3,14.180 ||
jātisaṃkhyāsamāhārairyathaiva sahacāriṇi  |
dravye kriyāḥ pravartanta ekātmatve vyapekṣite  || 3,14.181 ||
mūrtibhyo mūrtidharmāṇāṃ tathābhedasya darśanāt |
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca kriyāyogaśca kalpate  || 3,14.182 ||
sāmānādhikaraṇye tu matublopādapekṣite  |
luktaddhitalukīti syālluktatrāpyupalakṣaṇam  || 3,14.183 ||
kesāṃ cittyaktabhedeṣu dravyeṣveva vidhīyate  |
saṃstyānavatsu ṭābādirabhedena samanvayāt || 3,14.184 ||
sāmānyabhūto dravyātmā paricchinnaparigrahaḥ  |
kriyābhiryujyate bhedairbhāgaśaścāvatiṣṭhate  || 3,14.185 ||
śuklādiṣvāśrayadravyaṃ prādhānyenābhidhīyate  |
strītvaṃ tu pratyayārthatvādabhidhāviṣayo yataḥ  || 3,14.186 ||
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhādāśrayaṃ pratipadyate  |
strītvaṃ svabhāvasiddho vā guṇabhāvaviparyayaḥ  || 3,14.187 ||
sākāṅkṣatvādguṇatvena sāmānyaṃ vopadiśyate  |
vyaktīnāmātmadharmo'sāvekaprakhyānibandhanaḥ  || 3,14.188 ||
evambhūtā ca sāvasthā bhāgabhedaparigrahe  |
kṛte buddhyaiva bhedānāmāśrayatve ca kalpite  || 3,14.189 ||
niskṛṣṭeṣvapi bhedeṣu vyaktirūpāśraye tataḥ  |
liṅgapratyavamarśena liṅgasaṃkhye prapadyate  || 3,14.190 ||
antarena caśabdasya prayogaṃ dvandvabhāvinām  |
aviśiṣṭārthavṛttitvaṃ rūpābhedātpratīyate  || 3,14.191 ||
vikalpavati vā vṛttirnivartye'tha samuccite  |
teṣāmajñātaśaktīnāṃ dyotakena niyamyate  || 3,14.192 ||
vṛttau viśiṣṭarūpatvāccaśabdo vinivartate  |
arthabhede'pi sārūpyāttaccārthenāpadiśyate  || 3,14.193 ||
casya cāsattvabhūto'rthaḥ sa evāśriyate yadi  |
taddharmatvaṃ tato dvandve cādiṣvarthakṛtaṃ hi tat || 3,14.194 ||
cārthaḥ śabde kva cidbhedātkathaṃ citsamavasthitaḥ  |
dyotakāścādayastasya vaktā dvandvastu tadvatām  || 3,14.195 ||
vikalpādyabhidheyasya cārthasyānyapadārthatā  |
dyotakatvānna kalpeta tasmātsadupalakṣyate  || 3,14.196 ||
tatra svābhāvikaṃ liṅgaṃ śabdadharme vyapekṣite  |
śabdaḥ kaścittamevārthaṃ kathaṃ citpratipadyate  || 3,14.197 ||
śabdādarthāḥ pratāyante sa bhedānāṃ vidhāyakaḥ  |
anumānaṃ vivakṣāyāḥ śabdādanyanna vidyate  || 3,14.198 ||
samuccitaḥ syāddvandvārtho guṇabhūtasamuccayaḥ  |
samuccayo vāpi bhavedguṇabhūtasamuccitaḥ  || 3,14.199 ||
samuccitasya prādhānye liṅgasaṃkhye svabhāvataḥ  |
samuccayasya prādhānye śāstraṃ syātpratipādakam  || 3,14.200 ||
samuccayavato'rthasya prādhānye'pyapare viduḥ  |
nimittānuvidhāyitvādasiddhiṃ liṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ  || 3,14.201 ||
samuccayo nimittaṃ cetsyānnimittānuvartanam  |
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ cārtho dvandvanibandhanaḥ  || 3,14.202 ||
samuccitanimittatve cārthasyāpagame'pi vā  |
svabhāvasiddhe dvandvasya liṅgasaṃkhye vyavasthite  || 3,14.203 ||
padāntarasthasyārthasya dyotakatvānna yujyate  |
nipāto liṅgasaṃkhyābhyāṃ dvandvastvarthasya vācakaḥ  || 3,14.204 ||
nimittānuvidhāne ca dravyadharmānapekṣaṇāt |
guṇapradhānabhāvena kriyāyogo na kalpate  || 3,14.205 ||
yasya nāsti kriyāyogaḥ svatantro'sau na vidyate  |
artho dvandvasya tatra syādupādānamanarthakam  || 3,14.206 ||
samuccayavato'rthasya vācako nānuvartate  |
nimittamapi cāsyārthaḥ svadharmairyujyate tataḥ  || 3,14.207 ||
bāhyo nāstyāśrayo dvandve viśeṣau tatra hi śrutau  |
samuccayastadādhārastaddharmairvyapadiśyate  || 3,14.208 ||
yo vāvayavabhedābhyāṃ bhedavadbhyāmivānvitaḥ  |
ekaḥ samūho dharmān sa bhāgayoḥ pratipadyate  || 3,14.209 ||
ekaśca dvyātmako'rtho'sau bhedābhedasamanvitaḥ  |
yau bhedāvāśritastatsthe liṅgasaṃkhye prapadyate  || 3,14.210 ||
yathā svaśabdābhihite caitrārthe na prayujyate  |
caitraśabdo bahuvrihāvaprayogastathā bhavet || 3,14.211 ||
yathā gauriti śuklāderabhidhānaṃ na vidyate  |
evaṃ yasyābhisaṃbandho gobhistāvatpratīyate  || 3,14.212 ||
saṃbandhī niyato rūḍhaścitrāṇāṃ na ca vidyate  |
gavāṃ yathā vajrapāṇistryakṣe vā'pi vyavasthitaḥ  || 3,14.213 ||
śabdāntaratvādvākyeṣu viśeṣā yadyapi śrutāḥ  |
vṛttiśabdo'nya evāyaṃ sāmānyasyābhidhāyakaḥ  || 3,14.214 ||
agoracitragoścaiva rūpabhedānnivartakaḥ  |
na citragurviśeṣāṇāṃ rūpābhedāttu vācakaḥ  || 3,14.215 ||
yathā citragurityetatprayukte na prayujyate  |
evaṃ yadi syātsāmānyaṃ tasya na syātpratiśrutiḥ  || 3,14.216 ||
sarvādayo viśeṣāstu pradeśānāṃ nivartakāḥ  |
yathā pradeśāḥ sāmānya- pradeśāntarabādhakāḥ  || 3,14.217 ||
vibhaktyarthābhidhānādvā ṣaṣṭhī nānuprayujyate  |
dravyasyānabhidhānāttu tacchabdo'nuprayujyate  || 3,14.218 ||
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ cenmatublopātprakalpate  |
matupo'pi tadarthatvādanavasthā prasajyate  || 3,14.219 ||
saṃbandhasya ca saṃbandī saṃbandho'nyaḥ prasajyate  |
vibhaktyarthapradhāne ca kriyāyogo na kalpate  || 3,14.220 ||
vibhaktyarthapradhānatvāttatastatreti na kriyā  |
dṛśyādiḥ karmakartrādi- nimittatvāya kalpate  || 3,14.221 ||
antarbhavecca saṃbandhaḥ prādhānyābhihitaḥ katham  |
sa prātipadikārthaśca tathābhūtaḥ kathaṃ bhatvet || 3,14.222 ||
asaṃbhavāttu saṃbandhe saṃbandhasahacāriṇi  |
jātisaṃkhyāsamāhāra- kāryāṇāmiva saṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.223 ||
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhādviśiṣṭāśrayavācinām  |
śuklādivalliṅgasaṃkhye śāstrārambhādbhaviṣyataḥ  || 3,14.224 ||
bhedena tu vivakṣāyāṃ sāmānye vā vivakṣite  |
saliṅgasya sasaṃkhyasya padārthasyāgatirbhavet || 3,14.225 ||
sādhutvaṃ na vibhaktyartha- mātre vṛttasya dṛśyate  |
kṛtsnārthavṛtteḥ sādhutvamityarthagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam  || 3,14.226 ||
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhāddravyavṛttirayaṃ yadā  |
saliṅgasya sasaṃkhyasya tadā sādhutvamucyate  || 3,14.227 ||
antarbhūtavibhaktyarthe ṣaṣṭhī na śrūyate yathā  |
tathāśrutiḥ prasajyeta liṅgasaṃkhyābhidhāyinām  || 3,14.228 ||
sādharmyamavyayena syādbahuvrīhestathā sati  |
liṅgasaṃkhyānimittasya saṃskārasyāpavartanāt || 3,14.229 ||
prayuktena ca saṃbandhāccaitrādiśravanaṃ bhavet |
vinā vibhaktyā saṃbandho vibhaktyā vidyate vinā  || 3,14.230 ||
abhidhāne'pi saṃkhyāyāḥ saṃkhyātvaṃ na nivartate  |
ṣaṣṭhyarthasyābhidhāne tu syātprātipadikārthatā  || 3,14.231 ||
anuprayogasiddhyarthaṃ na vibhaktyarthakalpanā  |
vastvantaramupakṣiptamiti ke citpracakṣate  || 3,14.232 ||
saṃbandibhirviśiṣṭānāṃ saṃbandhānāṃ nimittatā  |
saṃbandhairvā viśiṣṭānāṃ tadvatāṃ syānnimittatā  || 3,14.233 ||
ke citsaṃyogino daṇḍādviṣāṇātsamavāyinaḥ  |
tadvati pratyayānāhurbahuvrīhiṃ tathaiva ca  || 3,14.234 ||
bhinnaṃ saṃbandhibhedena saṃbandhamapare viduḥ  |
nimittaṃ sa vibhaktyarthaḥ samāsenābhidhīyate  || 3,14.235 ||
pradhānamanyārthatayā bhinnaṃ svairupasarjanaiḥ  |
nimittamabbidheyaṃ vā sarvapaścādapekṣyate  || 3,14.236 ||
svāmini vyatirekaśca vākye yadyapi dṛśyate  |
prādhānya eva tasyeṣṭo bahuvrīhirvivakṣite  || 3,14.237 ||
gavāṃ viśeṣaṇatvena yadā tadvān pravartate  |
asyaitā iti tatrārthe bahuvrīhirna vidyate  || 3,14.238 ||
yadā pratyavamarśastu tāsāṃ svāmī gavāmiti  |
gobhistadābhisaṃbandho nimittatvāya kalpate  || 3,14.239 ||
apekṣamānaḥ saṃbandhaṃ rūḍhitvasya nivṛttaye  |
nimittānuvidhāyitvāttaddharmārthaḥ prasajyate  || 3,14.240 ||
nānā citrā iti yathā nimittamanurudhyate  |
nānābhūte'pi vṛttaḥ san bahuvrīhistathā bhavet || 3,14.241 ||
saṃbandhini nimitte tu dravyadharmo na hīyate  |
liṅgābhāvo hi liṅgasya virodhitvena vartate  || 3,14.242 ||
saṃkhvāvāṃlliṅgavāmścārtho'bhinnadharmā, nimittataḥ  |
āsanna eva dravyatvāttaddharmairna virudhyate  || 3,14.243 ||
vibhaktyarthena cāviṣṭaṃ śuddhaṃ ceti dvidhā sthitam  |
dravyaṃ śuddhasya yo dharmaḥ sa na syādanyadharmaṇaḥ  || 3,14.244 ||
dravyamātrasya nirdeśe bhedo'yamavivakṣitaḥ  |
granthe pūrvatra bhedastu dvitīye'nupradarśitaḥ  || 3,14.245 ||
dravyasya grahaṇaṃ cātra liṅgasaṃkhyāviśeṣaṇam  |
dravyāśritatvaṃ hi tayostato'nyasya na sidhyataḥ  || 3,14.246 ||
saṃbandhibhinnasaṃbandha- parichinne pravartate  |
samāso dravyasāmānye viśiṣṭārthānupātini  || 3,14.247 ||
dravyadharmānatikrānto bhedadharmeṣvavasthitaḥ  |
bhaviṣyadāśrayāpekṣe liṅgasaṃkhye prapadyate  || 3,14.248 ||
śāstrapravṛttibhede'pi laukiko'rtho na bhidyate  |
nañsamase yatastatra trayaḥ pakṣā vicāritāḥ  || 3,14.249 ||
śabdāntare'pi caikatvamāśrityaivaṃ vicāraṇā  |
abrahmaṇādiṣu nañaḥ prayogo na hi vidyate  || 3,14.250 ||
prāksamāsātpadārthānāṃ nivṛttirdyotyate nañā  |
svabhāvato nivṛttānāṃ rūpābhedādalakṣitā  || 3,14.251 ||
brāhmaṇādisthayā vākyeṣvākhyātapadavācyayā  |
kriyayā yasya saṃbandho vṛttistasya na vidyate  || 3,14.252 ||
pācakādipadasthā cennañā saṃbadhyate kriyā  |
tatra sattānupādānāttripakṣī nopapadyate  || 3,14.253 ||
sattayaivābhisaṃbandho yadi sarvatra kalpyate  |
asanniti samāse'smin sattānyā parikalpyatām  || 3,14.254 ||
ktvānte ca tumunante ca nañsamāse na dṛśyate  |
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatvaṃ nañāsattābhidhāyinā  || 3,14.255 ||
kriyāyāḥ sādhanādhāra- sāmānye nañvyavasthitaḥ  |
tato viśiṣṭairādhārairyujyate brāhmaṇādibhiḥ  || 3,14.256 ||
vṛttau yathā gatādyarthamupādāya nirādayaḥ  |
yujyante sādhanādhārairnañsamāse'pi sa kramaḥ  || 3,14.257 ||
tatrāsati naño vṛtterbrāhmaṇakṣatriyādibhiḥ  |
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatvaṃ kalpyate kubjakhañjavat || 3,14.258 ||
kāmacāre ca satyevamasataḥ syātpradhānatā,  |
guṇatvamitareṣāṃ ca teṣāṃ vā syātpradhānatā  || 3,14.259 ||
prādhānyenāśritāḥ pūrvaṃ śruteḥ sāmānyavṛttayaḥ  |
viśeṣa eva prakrāntā brāhmaṇakṣatrivādavaḥ  || 3,14.260 ||
yathā gaurādibhisteṣāmavacchedo vidhīyate  |
asatāpyanabhivyaktaṃ tādātmyaṃ vyajyate tathā  || 3,14.261 ||
yathā sattābhidhānāya sannarthaḥ parikalpyate  |
tathāsattābhidhānāya nirupākhyo'pi kalpate  || 3,14.262 ||
kṣatriyādau padaṃ kṛtvā buddhiḥ sattāntarāśrayā  |
jātyā bhinnāṃ tataḥ sattāṃ prasaktāmapakarṣati  || 3,14.263 ||
abhāva iti bhāvasya pratiṣedhe vivakṣite  |
sopākhyatvamanāśritya pratiṣedho na kalpate  || 3,14.264 ||
anekadharmavacanāḥ śabdāḥ saṅghābhidhāyinaḥ  |
ekadeśeṣu vartante tulyarūpāḥ svabhāvataḥ  || 3,14.265 ||
yathaikadeśakaraṇātkṛta itvabhidhīyate  |
akṛtaśceti saṃghātaḥ sa evābrāhmaṇe kramaḥ  || 3,14.266 ||
brāhmaṇo'brāhmaṇastasmādupanyāsātprasajyate  |
akṛte vā kṛtāsaṅgādaviśiṣṭaṃ kṛtākṛtāt || 3,14.267 ||
amukhyasaṃbhave tatra mukhyasya vinivṛttaye  |
śāstrānvākhyānasamaye nañprayukto viśeṣakaḥ  || 3,14.268 ||
padārthānupaghātena dṛśyate'nyaviśeṣaṇam  |
atha jātimato'rthasya kaściddharmo nivartitaḥ  || 3,14.269 ||
avaśyaṃ brāhmaṇe kaścitkva ciddharmo na vidyate  |
viśeṣāvacanāttatra nañaḥ śrutiranarthikā  || 3,14.270 ||
aviśiṣṭasya paryāyo nañviśiṣṭaḥ prasajyate  |
anvākhyānāddhi sādhutvamevaṃbhūte pratīyate  || 3,14.271 ||
padārthānupaghātena yadyapyatra viśeṣaṇam  |
upacārasato'rthasya sāvasthā dyotyate nañā  || 3,14.272 ||
viśeṣyeṣu yathābhūtaḥ padārthaḥ samavasthitaḥ  |
tathābhūte tathābhāvo gamyate bhedahetubhiḥ  || 3,14.273 ||
nivṛtte'vayavastasmin padārthe vartate katham  |
nānimittā hi śabdasya pravṛttirupapadyate  || 3,14.274 ||
ārācchabdavadekasya viruddhe'rthe svabhāvataḥ  |
śabdasya vṛttiryadyasti nañaḥ śrutiranarthikā  || 3,14.275 ||
atha svabhāvo vacanādanvākhyeyatvamarhati  |
tadvācyamaprasiddhatvānnañārtho vinivartyate  || 3,14.276 ||
yadyapyubhayavṛttitvaṃ pradhānaṃ tu pratīyate  |
prasthānaṃ gamyate śuddhe tadarthe'pi na tiṣṭhatau  || 3,14.277 ||
kimarthamatathābhūte'sati mukhyārthasaṃbhave  |
bhede brāhmaṇaśabdasya vṛttirabhyupagamyate  || 3,14.278 ||
ayaṃ padārtha etasmin kṣatriyādau na vidyate  |
iti tadvacanaḥ śabdaḥ pratyayāya prayujyate  || 3,14.279 ||
buddherviṣayatāṃ prāpte śabdādarthe pratīyate  |
pravṛttirvā nivṛttirvā grutyā hyartho'nusajyate  || 3,14.280 ||
asamyagupadeśādvā nimittātsaṃśayasya vā  |
śabdapravṛttirna tvasti loṣṭādiṣu viparyayāt || 3,14.281 ||
anekasmādasa iti prādhānye sati sidhyati  |
sāpekṣatvaṃ pradhānānāmevaṃ yuktaṃ tvatalvidhau  || 3,14.282 ||
ekasya ca pradhānatvāttadviśeṣaṇasaṃnidhau  |
pradhānadharmāvyāvṛttirato na vacanāntaram  || 3,14.283 ||
pradhānamatra bhedyatvādekārtho vikṛto nañā  |
hitvā svadharmān vartante dvyādayo'pyekatāṃ gatāḥ  || 3,14.284 ||
brāhmaṇatvaṃ yathāpannā nañyuktāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ  |
dvitvādiṣu tathaikatvaṃ nañyogādupacaryate  || 3,14.285 ||
ekatvayogamāsādya sa dharmaḥ pratiṣidhyate  |
dvyādibhyasteṣu tacchabdo vartate brāhmaṇādivat || 3,14.286 ||
āviṣṭasaṃkhyo vākye'sau yathā dvyādau prayujyate  |
vṛttau tasya pradhānatvātsā saṃkhyā na nivartate  || 3,14.287 ||
pratiṣedhyo yathābhūtastathābhūto'nuṣajyate  |
vacanāntarayoge hi na so'rthaḥ pratiṣidhyate  || 3,14.288 ||
aśukla iti kṛṣṇādiryathārthaḥ saṃpratīyate  |
saṃkhyāntaraṃ tathāneka ityatrāpyabhidhīyate  || 3,14.289 ||
kriyāprasaṅgātsarveṣu karmasvaṅgīkṛteṣu ca  |
ekasmin pratiṣiddhe'pi prāptamanyatpratīyate  || 3,14.290 ||
kriyāśrutiśca prakrānte prasajyapratiṣedhane  |
paryudāse tu niyataṃ saṃkhyeyāntaramucyate  || 3,14.291 ||
dhātvarthaḥ karmaviṣayo vyapadiṣṭaḥ svasādhanaiḥ  |
arthātsarvāṇi karmāṇi prāgākṣipyāvatiṣṭhate  || 3,14.292 ||
nirjñātasādhanādhāre yatrākhyāte prayujyate  |
aneka iti paścācca tiṣṭhatītyanuṣajyate  || 3,14.293 ||
sādhyatvāttatra siddhena kriyā dravyeṇa lakṣyate  |
prāgevāṅgīkṛtaṃ dravyamataḥ pūrveṇa bhidyate  || 3,14.294 ||
saṃkhyaiva pratiṣedhena saṃkhyāntaramapekṣate  |
vākye'pi tena naikatva- mātrameva nivartyate  || 3,14.295 ||
snehāntarādavacchedastathāsatteḥ pratīyate  |
tailena bhojane'prāpte na tvanyadupasecanam  || 3,14.296 ||
ekārthe vartamānābhyāmasatā brāhmaṇena ca  |
yadā jātyantaraṃ bāhyaṃ kṣatriyādyapadiśyate  || 3,14.297 ||
śyāmeva śastrī kanyeti yathānyadvyapadiśyate  |
asan brāhmaṇa ityābhyāṃ tathānye kṣatriyādayaḥ  || 3,14.298 ||
asāsno gauriti yathā, gavayo vyapadiśyate  |
jātyantaraṃ na goreva sasnābhāvaḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.299 ||
tulyarūpaṃ yathākhyātaṃ kaṇṭakairbhedahetubhiḥ  |
khadiraṃ jātibhedena kharjūrātpratipadyate  || 3,14.300 ||
avidyamānabrāhmaṇyo yādṛśo brāhmaṇo bhavet |
aṅgīkṛtopamānena tathānyo'rtho'bhidhīyate  || 3,14.301 ||
avṛṣṭayo yathā varsā nīhārābhrasamāvṛtāḥ  |
tadrūpatvātsa hemanta ityabhinnaḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.302 ||
apare brāhmaṇādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ jātivācinām  |
dravyasyānyapadārthatve nañā yogaṃ pracakṣate  || 3,14.303 ||
na caivaṃviṣayaḥ kaścidbahuvrīhiḥ prakalpate  |
aguraśva iti vyāptirnañsamāsena yasya na  || 3,14.304 ||
dvandvaikadeśinoruktā paravalliṅgatā yataḥ  |
avarṣāsu tato'siddhiriṣṭayorliṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ  || 3,14.305 ||
viśeṣaṇaṃ brāhmaṇādi kriyāsaṃbandhino'sataḥ  |
yadā viṣayabhinnaṃ tattadāsattvaṃ pratīyate  || 3,14.306 ||
brāhmaṇatvena cāsattvāducyate sattadanyathā  |
asadityapi sattvena sataḥ sattā nivartyate  || 3,14.307 ||
samanyadravyavṛttitvānnimittānuvidhāyinaḥ  |
ayogo liṅgasaṃkhyābhyāṃ syādvā sāmānyadharmatā  || 3,14.308 ||
prāgasattvābhidhāyitvaṃ samāse dravyavācitā  |
nimittānuvidhānaṃ ca na sarvatra svabhāvataḥ  || 3,14.309 ||
nimittānuvidhāne ca kriyāyogo na kalpate  |
tathā cāvyapadeśyatvādupādānamanarthakam  || 3,14.310 ||
asatsāmānyavṛttirvā viśeṣaiḥ kṣatriyādibhiḥ  |
prayuktairāśrayairbhinno yāti talliṅgasaṃkhyatām  || 3,14.311 ||
prāgāśrayo hi bhedāya pradhāne'bhyantarīkṛtaḥ  |
punaḥ pratyavamarśena vibhakta iva dṛśyate  || 3,14.312 ||
samāse śrūyate svārtho yena tadvāṃstadāśrayaḥ  |
dravyaṃ tu liṅgasaṃkhyāvadasatābhyantarīkṛtam  || 3,14.313 ||
ekārthaviṣayau śabdau tasminnanyārthavartinau  |
asataiva tu bhedānāṃ sarveṣāmupasaṃgrahaḥ  || 3,14.314 ||
te kṣatriyādibhirvācyā vācyā vā sarvanāmabhiḥ  |
yāntīvānyapadārthatvaṃ naño rūpāvikalpanāt || 3,14.315 ||
viśeṣasyāprayoge tu liṅgasaṃkhye na sidhyataḥ  |
avarṣādiṣu dosaśca hemanto'nyāśrayo yataḥ  || 3,14.316 ||
ākṛtiḥ sarvaśabdānāṃ yadā vācyā pratīyate  |
ekatvādekaśabdatvaṃ nyāyyaṃ tasyāśca varṇyate  || 3,14.317 ||
āviṣṭaliṅgatā tasyāṃ syādgrāmyapagusaṅghavat |
dravyabhede'pi caikatvāttatraikavacanaṃ bhavet || 3,14.318 ||
āśrayāṇāṃ hi liṅgaiḥ sā niyataireva yujyate  |
tathā ca yuktavadbhāve pratiṣedho nirarthakaḥ  || 3,14.319 ||
sarvatrāviṣṭaliṅgatvaṃ lokaliṅgaparigrahe  |
virodhitvātprasajyeta nāśritaṃ tacca laukikam  || 3,14.320 ||
sāmānyamākṛtirbhāvo jātirityatra laukikam  |
liṅgaṃ na saṃbhavatyeva tenānyatparigṛhyate  || 3,14.321 ||
pravṛttiriti sāmānyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tasya kathyate  |
āvirbhāvastirobhāvaḥ sthitiścetyatha bhidyate  || 3,14.322 ||
pravṛttimantaḥ sarve'rthāstisṛbhiśca pravṛttibhiḥ  |
satataṃ na viyujyante vācaścaivātra saṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.323 ||
yaścāpravṛttidharmārthaścitirūpeṇa gṛhyate  |
anuyātīva so'nyeṣāṃ pravṛttīrviśvagāśrayāḥ  || 3,14.324 ||
tenāsya citirūpaṃ ca citikālaśca bhidyate  |
tasya svarūpabhedastu na kaścidapi vidyate  || 3,14.325 ||
acetaneṣu caitanyaṃ saṃkrāntamiva dṛśyate  |
pratibimbakadharmeṇa yattacchabdanibandhanam  || 3,14.326 ||
avasthā tādṛśī nāsti yā liṅgena na yujyate  |
kva cittu śabdasaṃskāro liṅgasyānāśraye sati  || 3,14.327 ||
kṛttaddhitābhidheyānāṃ bhāvānāṃ na virudhyate  |
śāstre liṅgaṃ guṇāvasthā tathā cākṛtiriṣyate  || 3,14.328 ||
liṅgaṃ prati na bhedo'sti dravyapakṣe'pi kaścana  |
tasmātsapta vikalpā ye saivātrāviṣṭaliṅgatā  || 3,14.329 ||
vacane niyamaḥ śāstrāddravyasyābhyupagamyate  |
yatastadākṛtau śāstramanyathaiva samarthyate  || 3,14.330 ||
vartate yo bahuṣvartho'bhede tasya vivakṣite  |
svāśrayairvyapadiṣṭasya śāstre vacanamucyate  || 3,14.331 ||
yadā tvāśrayabhedena bheda eva pratīyate  |
ākṛterdravyapakṣena tadā bhedo na vidyate  || 3,14.332 ||
abhede tvekaśabdatvācchāstrācca vacane sati  |
ekaśeṣo na vaktavyo vacanānāṃ ca saṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.333 ||
nanu cānabhidheyatve dravyasya tadapāśrayaḥ  |
ākṛterupakāro'yaṃ dravyābhāvānna kalpate  || 3,14.334 ||
vyapadeśo'bhidheyena na śāstre kaścidāśritaḥ  |
dravyaṃ nāma padārtho yo na ca sa pratiṣidhyate  || 3,14.335 ||
guṇabhāvo'bhidheyatvaṃ prati dravyasya nāśritaḥ  |
upakāri guṇaḥ śeṣaḥ parārtha iti kalpanā  || 3,14.336 ||
dravye na guṇabhāvo'sti vinādravyābhidhāyitām  |
ākṛtau vā pradhānatvamata evaṃ samarthyate  || 3,14.337 ||
kaiścidguṇapradhānatvaṃ nāmākhyātavadiṣyate  |
na vṛttivatparārthasya guṇabhāvastu varṇyate  || 3,14.338 ||
guṇabhūtasya nānātvādākṛterekaśabdatā  |
siddho vacanabhedaśca dravyabhedasamanvayāt || 3,14.339 ||
sādhanaṃ guṇabhāvena kriyāyā bhedakaṃ yathā  |
ākhyāteṣvekaśabdāyā jāterdravyaṃ tathocyate  || 3,14.340 ||
ekatve tulyarūpatvācchabdānāṃ pratipādane  |
nimittāttadvato'rthasya viśiṣṭagrahaṇe sati  || 3,14.341 ||
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhādāśrayairākṛteḥ saha  |
pravṛttau bhinnaśabdāyāṃ liṅgasaṃkhye prasidhyataḥ  || 3,14.342 ||
prākca jātyabhisaṃbandhātsarvanāmābhidheyatā  |
vastūpalakṣaṇaṃ sattve prayujyante tyadādayaḥ  || 3,14.343 ||
pākau pākā iti yathā bhedakaḥ kaiścidāśrayaḥ  |
iṣyate cānupādāno dharmo'sau guṇavācinām  || 3,14.344 ||
āśrayasyānupādāne kevalaṃ labhate yadi  |
ādhāradharmān sāmānyaṃ purastāttadvicāritam  || 3,14.345 ||
jātau pūrvaṃ pravṛttānāṃ śabdānāṃ jātivācinām  |
aśabdavācyātsaṃbandhādvyaktirapyupajāyate  || 3,14.346 ||
so'yamityabhisaṃbandhājjātidharmopacaryate  |
dravyaṃ tadāśrayo bhedo jāteścābhyupagamyate  || 3,14.347 ||
mañcaśabdo yathādheyaṃ mañceṣveva vyavasthitaḥ  |
tattvenāha tathā jāti- śabdo dravyeṣu vartate  || 3,14.348 ||
tatra jātipadārthatvaṃ tathaivābhyupagamyate  |
jātirutsṛṣṭasaṃkhyā tu dravyātmanyanuṣajyate  || 3,14.349 ||
asyedamiti vā yatra so'yamityapi vā śrutiḥ  |
vartate paradharmeṇa tadanyadabhidhīyate  || 3,14.350 ||
yatpradhānaṃ na tasyāsti svarūpamanirūpanāt |
guṇasya cātmanā dravyaṃ tadbhāvenopalakṣyate  || 3,14.351 ||
guṇasya bhedakāle tu prādhānyamupajāyate  |
saṃsargaśrutirartheṣu sākṣādeva na vartate  || 3,14.352 ||
jātau vṛtto yadā dravye sa śabdo vartate punaḥ  |
jātereva padārthatvaṃ na tadābhyupagamyate  || 3,14.353 ||
pravṛttānāṃ punarvṛttirekatvenopavarṇyate  |
pratipatterupāyeṣu na tattvamanugamyate  || 3,14.354 ||
apṛthakśabdavācyasya jātirāśrīyate yadā  |
dravyasya sati saṃsparśe tadā jātipadārthatā  || 3,14.355 ||
dravyasya sati saṃsparśe dravyamāśrīyate yadā  |
vācyaṃ tenaiva śabdena tadā dravyapadārthatā  || 3,14.356 ||
apṛthakśabdavācyāpi bhedamātre pravartate  |
yadā saṃbandhavajjātiḥ sāpi dravyapadārthatā  || 3,14.357 ||
atyantabhinnayoreva jātidravyābhidhāyinoḥ  |
avācyasyopakāritva āśrite tūbhayārthatā  || 3,14.358 ||
āśrite tvāśrayakṛtaṃ bhedamabhyupagacchatā  |
punaścāpyekaśabdatvaṃ jātiśabde'nuvarṇitam  || 3,14.359 ||
anirjātasya nirjñānaṃ yena tanmānamucyate  |
prasthādi tena meyātmā sākalyenāvadhāryate  || 3,14.360 ||
anirjñātaṃ prasiddhena yena taddharma gamyate  |
sākalyenāparijñānādupamānaṃ taducyate  || 3,14.361 ||
dvayoḥ samānayordharma upamānopameyayoḥ  |
samāsa upamānānāṃ śabdaistadabhidhāyibhiḥ  || 3,14.362 ||
ādhārabhedādbhedo yaḥ śyāmatve so'vivakṣitaḥ  |
guṇo'sāvāśritaikatvo bhinnādhāraḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.363 ||
guṇayorniyato bhedo guṇajātestathaikatā  |
ekatve'tyantabhede vā, nopamānasya saṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.364 ||
jātimātravyapekṣāyāmupamārtho na kaścana  |
śyāmatvamekaṃ guṇayorubhayorapi vartate  || 3,14.365 ||
yenaiva hetunā śyāmā śastrī tatra pratīyate  |
sa heturdevadattāyāḥ pratyaye na viśiṣyate  || 3,14.366 ||
āśrayādyo guṇe bhedo jāteryā cāviśiṣṭatā  |
tābhyāmubhābhyāṃ dravyātmā savyāpāraḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.367 ||
so'yamekatvanānātve vyavahāraḥ samāśritaḥ  |
bhedābhedavimarśena vyatikīrṇena vartate  || 3,14.368 ||
śyāmetyevābhidhiyeta jātimātre vivakṣite  |
śastryādināmupādāne tatra nāsti prayojanam  || 3,14.369 ||
aśabdavācyo yo bhedaḥ śyāmamātre na vartate  |
śyāmeṣu keṣu cidvṛttiryasya so'tra vyapekṣyate  || 3,14.370 ||
śyāmeṣu keṣu citkiṃ citkiṃ citsarvatra vartate  |
sāmānyaṃ kaścidekasmiñchyāme bhedo vyavasthitaḥ  || 3,14.371 ||
tathā hi sati saurabhye bhedo jātyutpalādiṣu  |
gandhānāṃ sati bhede tu sādṛśyamupalabhyate  || 3,14.372 ||
guṇānāmāśrayādbhedaḥ svato vāpyanugamyate  |
anirdeśyādviśeṣādvā saṃkarādvā guṇāntaraiḥ  || 3,14.373 ||
upamānaṃ prasiddhatvātsarvatra vyatiricyate  |
upameyatvamādhikye sāmye vā na nivartate  || 3,14.374 ||
anyaistu mānaṃ jātyādi bhedyasyārthasya varṇyate  |
anirjñātasvarūpo hi jñeyo'rthastena mīyate  || 3,14.375 ||
mitastu svena mānena prasiddho yo guṇāśrayaḥ  |
āśrayāntaramānāya svadharmeṇa pravartate  || 3,14.376 ||
rūpāntareṇa saṃsparśo rūpāntaravatāṃ satām  |
bhinnena yasya bhedyānāmupamānaṃ taducyate  || 3,14.377 ||
dharmaḥ samānaḥ śyāmādirupamānopameyayoḥ  |
āśriyamānaprādhānyo dharmeṇānyena bhidyate  || 3,14.378 ||
śastrīkumāryoḥ sadṛśaḥ śyāma ityevamāśrite  |
vyapadeśyamaneneti nimittaṃ guṇayoḥ sthitam  || 3,14.379 ||
yadā nimittaistadvanto gacchantīva tadātmatām  |
bhedāśrayaṃ tadākhyānamupamānopameyayoḥ  || 3,14.380 ||
tattvāsaṅgavivakṣāyāṃ yeṣu bhedo nivartate  |
luptopamāni tānyāhustaddharmeṇa samāśrayāt || 3,14.381 ||
śastryāṃ prasiddhaṃ śyāmatvaṃ mānaṃ sā tena mīyate  |
anyā śyāmā tu tadrūpā tenātyantaṃ na mīyate  || 3,14.382 ||
śastriṃ svena guṇenāto mimānāmāśrayāntaram  |
asamāptaguṇaṃ siddherupamānaṃ pracakṣate  || 3,14.383 ||
upameye sthito dharmaḥ śruto'nyatrānumīyate  |
śruto'tha vopamānastha upameye'numiyate  || 3,14.384 ||
adhīyate brāhmaṇavatkṣatriyā iti dṛśyate  |
upameyasya bhinnatvādvacanaṃ kṣatriyāśrayam  || 3,14.385 ||
sādhāraṇaṃ bruvan dharma kva cideva vyavasthitam  |
sāmānyavacanaḥ śabda iti sūtre'padiśyate  || 3,14.386 ||
nābhedena na bhedena guṇo dviṣṭho'bhidhīyate  |
bhinnayordharmayorekaḥ śrūyate'nyaḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.387 ||
nātyantāya mimīte yatsāmānye samavasthitam  |
sādṛśyādupameyārtha- samīpe parikalpyate  || 3,14.388 ||
mānaṃ prati samīpaṃ vā sādṛśyena pratīyate  |
paricchedāddhi sādṛśyamiha mānopamānayoḥ  || 3,14.389 ||
ekajātivyapekṣāyāṃ tadevetyavasīyate  |
bhedasyaiva vyapekṣāyāmanyadeveti gamyate  || 3,14.390 ||
karmatvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ ca bhedenaivāśritaṃ yataḥ  |
atyantaikatvaviṣayo na syāttenātra samśayaḥ  || 3,14.391 ||
bhede'pi tulyarūpatvācchālīmṃ tāniti dṛśyate  |
jātyabhedātsa evāyamiti bhinno'bhidhīyate  || 3,14.392 ||
kathaṃ hyavayavo'nyasya syādanya iti cocyate  |
atyantabhede nānātvaṃ yatra tattvaṃ na vidyate  || 3,14.393 ||
abhedasya vivakṣāyāmekatvaṃ saṅghasaṅghinoḥ  |
saṅghinorna tvabhedo'sti tathānyatvamudāhṛtam  || 3,14.394 ||
tatrābhinnavyapekṣāyāmupamārtho na vidyate  |
yo hi gauriti vijñāne hetuḥ so'sti gavāntare  || 3,14.395 ||
vyāvṛttānāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ vyāpāre tu vivakṣite  |
na kaścidupakāro'sti buddherbuddhyantaraṃ prati  || 3,14.396 ||
kiṃ cidyatrāsti sāmānyaṃ yadi bhedāśca ke cana  |
gotvaṃ goṣvasti sāmānyaṃ bhedāśca śabalādayaḥ  || 3,14.397 ||
sāmānyaṃ śyāmatānyaiva taddhi sādhāraṇaṃ dvayoḥ  |
tadeva siddhyasiddhibhyāṃ bheda ityapadiśyate  || 3,14.398 ||
śyāmatvameva sāmānyamanyeṣāmubhayoḥ sthitam  |
saṃpūrnatvāttadanyasmādviśeṣa iti gamyate  || 3,14.399 ||
ākṛtau vāpi sāmānye kva cideva vyavasthitāḥ  |
śyāmādau ye'vasīyante viśeṣāsta ihāśritāḥ  || 3,14.400 ||
jāterabhede bhede vā sādṛśyaṃ tatpracakṣate  |
kaścitkadā citarthātmā tathābhūto'padiśyate  || 3,14.401 ||
yatrārthe pratyayābhedo na kadā cidvikalpate  |
avidyamānabhedatvātsa eka iti gamyate  || 3,14.402 ||
yo'rtha āśritanānātvaḥ sa evetyapadiśyate  |
vyāpāraṃ jātibhāgasya tatrāpi pratijānate  || 3,14.403 ||
jātibhāgāśrayā prakhyā tatrābhinnā pravartate  |
vyaktibhāgāśrayā buddhistatra bhedena jāyate  || 3,14.404 ||
anyatra vartamānaṃ sadbhedābhedasamanvitam  |
nimittaṃ punaranyatra nānātveneva gṛhyate  || 3,14.405 ||
ādhāreṣu padanyāsaṃ kṛtvopaiti tadāśrayam  |
sa sādṛśyasya viṣaya ityanyairapadiśyate  || 3,14.406 ||
parāpekṣe yathā bhāve kāraṇākhyā pravartate  |
tathānyādhigamāpekṣamupamānaṃ pracakṣate  || 3,14.407 ||
gurugiśyapitāputra- kriyākālādayo yathā  |
vyavahārāstathaupamyamapyapekṣānibandhanam  || 3,14.408 ||
śyāmatvamupamāne cedvṛttaṃ vṛttau prayujyate  |
upameyaṃ samāsena bāhyaṃ tatrābhidhīyate  || 3,14.409 ||
ṭābanta eva caitrādau śyāmāśabdastathā bhavet |
sūtre ca prathamābhāvānna śyāmādyupasarjanam  || 3,14.410 ||
atha tvekavibhaktitvādguṇatvādvopasarjanam  |
naivaṃ tittirikalmāṣyāmiṣṭaḥ strīpratyayo bhavet || 3,14.411 ||
satiśiṣṭabalīyastvādbāhye ṅiṣi ca satyapi  |
upamānasvaro na syāttasmātstryantaḥ samasyate  || 3,14.412 ||
guṇe na copamānasthe sāpekṣatvaṃ prakalpate  |
pradhānasya tathā na syādvyāghrādau liṅgadarśanam  || 3,14.413 ||
tasmātsati guṇatve'pi prādhānyaṃ vigrahāntare  |
naivaṃjātīyakaṃ śāstre saṃbhavatyupasarjanam  || 3,14.414 ||
upameyātmani śyāmo vartamāno'bhidhīyate  |
upamāneṣvanirdiṣṭaḥ sāmarthyātsa pratīyate  || 3,14.415 ||
dravyamātre'pi nirdiṣṭe candravaktre'nugamyate  |
viśiṣṭa eva candrastho guṇo nopaplavādayaḥ  || 3,14.416 ||
bhedabhāvanayaitacca samāse'pyupavarṇyate  |
viśiṣṭaguṇabhinne'rthe padamanyatprayujyate  || 3,14.417 ||
yadi bhinnādhikaraṇo vacanādanugamyate  |
mṛgīva capaletyatra puṃvadbhāvo na sidhyati  || 3,14.418 ||
astrīpūrvapadatvāttu puṃvadbhāvo bhaviṣyati  |
yathaiva mṛgadugdhādau na cetstryartho vivakṣyate  || 3,14.419 ||
śastrīva śastrīśyāmeti devadattaiva kathyate  |
tasyāmevobhayaṃ tasmāducyate śāstravigrahe  || 3,14.420 ||
puṃvadbhāvasya siddhyarthaṃ pakṣe strīpratyayasya ca  |
bahvapekṣyamatastasyāmubhayapratipādanam  || 3,14.421 ||
śyāmā śastrī yathā śyāmā śastrīkalpeti cocyate  |
tatropamānetarayoḥ śyāmetyetadapekṣyate  || 3,14.422 ||
atha śyāmeva śastrīyaṃ śyāmetyevaṃ prayujyate  |
śastrī yatheyaṃ śyāmeti tāvadeva pratīyate  || 3,14.423 ||
upalakṣaṇamātrārthā guṇasyāsya yadi śrutiḥ  |
pṛthagdvayoḥ śruto'pyeṣa neṣṭasvārthasya vācakaḥ  || 3,14.424 ||
upameyaṃ tu yadvācyaṃ tasya cetpratipādane  |
savyāpārā guṇāstatra sarvasyoktiḥ sakṛcchrutau  || 3,14.425 ||
prakārādhārabhedena viśeṣe samavasthitaḥ  |
śabdāntarābhisaṃbandhe sāmānyavacanaḥ katham  || 3,14.426 ||
sādṛśyamātraṃ sāmānyaṃ dviṣṭhaṃ kaiścitpratīyate  |
guṇo bhede'pyabhedena dvivṛttirvā vivakṣitaḥ  || 3,14.427 ||
vyāpāro jātibhāgasya dravyayorvābhidhitsitaḥ  |
rūpātsāmānyavācitvaṃ prāgvā vṛtterudāhṛtam  || 3,14.428 ||
vyāghraśabdo yadā śauryātpuruṣārthe'vatiṣṭhate  |
tadādhikaraṇābhedātsamāsasyāsti saṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.429 ||
śūraśabdaprayoge tu vyāghraśabdo mṛge sthitaḥ  |
bhinne'dhikaraṇe vṛttestatra naivāsti saṃbhavaḥ  || 3,14.430 ||
sāmānādhikaraṇye'pi guṇabhedasya saṃbhavāt |
prayogaḥ śūraśabdasya samāse'pyanuṣajyate  || 3,14.431 ||
pūjopādhiśca yo dṛṣṭaḥ kutsanopādhayaśca ye  |
teṣāṃ bhinnanimittatvānniyamārthā punaḥ śrutiḥ  || 3,14.432 ||
asaṃbhave'pi vā vṛtteḥ syādetalliṅgadarśanam  |
acveriti yathā liṅgamabhāve'pi bhṛśādiṣu  || 3,14.433 ||
vatyantāvayave vākye yadaupamyaṃ pratīyate  |
tatpratyayavidhau sūtre nirdeśo'yaṃ vicāryate  || 3,14.434 ||
kriyetyupādhiḥ prāthamyātprakṛtyarthasya yadyapi  |
na prātipadikaṃ tatra kriyāvācyupapadyate  || 3,14.435 ||
sattvavṛttasya śeṣe vā tṛtīyā sādhane'pi vā  |
tiṅāmasattvavācitvādubhayaṃ tanna vidyate  || 3,14.436 ||
pākādayastṛtīyāntāḥ sattvadharmasamanvayāt |
na kriyetyapadiśyante kṛtvo'rthapratyaye yathā  || 3,14.437 ||
ye cāvyayakṛtaḥ ke citkriyādharmasamanvitāḥ  |
teṣāmasattvavācitvaṃ tiṅantairna viśiṣyate  || 3,14.438 ||
kṛtvasujviṣayā yāpi śayitavyādiṣu kriyā  |
upamānopameyatvaṃ tatrātyantamasaṃbhavi  || 3,14.439 ||
na kevalau dravyaguṇau tadvān vāpyupamīyate  |
śayitavyādibhisteṣu nopamārtho'sti kaścana  || 3,14.440 ||
upamānopameyatve dravye cānuktadharmiṇi  |
nimittatvena gamyante rūḍhayogāḥ kriyāguṇāḥ  || 3,14.441 ||
hotavyasadṛśo hotetyatrāpyartho na vidyate  |
virodhātkriyayā tasmātkriyāvānnopamīyate  || 3,14.442 ||
kriyā samānajātiyā tadbhāvānnopamīyate  |
jātibhede'pi pākena bhinnāḥ pākādayaḥ kriyāḥ  || 3,14.443 ||
ādhārabhedādbhinnāyāmupamānasya saṃbhavaḥ  |
adhyetavyena viprāṇāṃ tulyamadhyayanaṃ viśām  || 3,14.444 ||
arthātprakaraṇādvāpi yatrāpekṣyaṃ pratīyate  |
sāmarthyādanapekṣasya tasya vṛttiḥ prasajyate  || 3,14.445 ||
tailapākena tulye ca ghṛtapāke vivakṣite  |
kriyāvadapi kāryāṇāṃ darśanātpratyayo bhavet || 3,14.446 ||
atiṅgrahaṇamevaṃ tu samāsasya nivartakam  |
gamanaṃ kārakasyeti ṇvulyanyasminna saṃbhavet || 3,14.447 ||
sarvasya parihārārthaṃ samudāyatvamāśritam  |
śuddhāyāḥ saṃbhavānna syātkriyāyā brāhmaṇādiṣu  || 3,14.448 ||
upamānavivakṣāyāṃ svadharmaśca nivartate  |
kriyāyā na śrutādyasmādupamānaṃ samāpyate  || 3,14.449 ||
tṛtīyo'pyāśrito bhedo dharmaḥ sādhāraṇo dvayoḥ  |
vyāpāravānna kṛtsnasya sāmyaṃ kṛtsnena vidyate  || 3,14.450 ||
dravye vāpi kriyāyāṃ vā nimittāttatprakalpate  |
kriyāṇāṃ vidyamānatvādvṛttirna syādgavādiṣu  || 3,14.451 ||
abhāvātkevalāyāstu tadvānarthaḥ pratīyate  |
pradhānāsaṃbhave yuktā lakṣaṇārthā kriyāśrutiḥ  || 3,14.452 ||
kriyāntareṣu sāpekṣāḥ kriyāśabdāḥ kriyāntare  |
upakārāya gṛhyante yathaiva brāhmaṇādayaḥ  || 3,14.453 ||
yathā prakarṣaḥ sarvatra nimittāntarahetukaḥ  |
dravyavadguṇaśabde'pi sa nimittamapekṣate  || 3,14.454 ||
yo ya uccāryate śabdaḥ sa svarūpanibandhanaḥ  |
yathā tathopamāneṣu vyapekṣa na nivartate  || 3,14.455 ||
kriyāvṛttestṛtīyāntasy- aivaṃ cāsaṃbhave sati  |
prasiddhanyāyakaraṇo bhāṣye yujirudāhṛtaḥ  || 3,14.456 ||
antarbhūte tu karaṇe prayogo na punarbhavet |
nyāyenāyuktamityatra jīvatau prāṇakarmavat || 3,14.457 ||
śāstrābhyāsācca bhedo'yamayuktamiti varṇyate  |
aśobhanamasaṃbaddhamiti rūḍhirvyavasthitā  || 3,14.458 ||
vivibhaktiḥ prakṛtyarthaṃ pratyupādhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
vibhaktipariṇāme ca prakalpyaṃ viṣayāntaram  || 3,14.459 ||
vibhaktyantarayogo hi yasya tadviṣayāntare  |
vibhaktyantarasaṃbandhaḥ sāmarthyādanumīyate  || 3,14.460 ||
sārūpyāttu tadevedamiti tatropacaryate  |
śabdāntaraṃ vibhaktyā tu yuktaṃ śāstre tadaśrutam  || 3,14.461 ||
prakṛtiścettṛtīyāntā tenetyasmātpratīyate  |
kriyeti prathamāntā sā kathaṃ bhavitumarhati  || 3,14.462 ||
kriyayeti tṛtīyā ca prayoge kasya kalpyatām  |
tenetyasya hi saṃbandhaḥ sūtrasthena na vidyate  || 3,14.463 ||
sopaskāreṣu sūtreṣu vākyaśeṣaḥ samarthyate  |
tena yattattṛtīyāntaṃ kriyā cetseti gamyate  || 3,14.464 ||
upādheḥ kasya cidvākye prayoga upalabhyate  |
pratīyamānadharmānyo na kadā citprayujyate  || 3,14.465 ||
nīlamutpalamityatra na viśeṣye na bhedake  |
kaścittaddharmavacano vākye śabdaḥ prayujyate  || 3,14.466 ||
atyantānugamāttatra na sūtre na ca vigrahe  |
vibhaktipariṇāmena kiṃ cidasti prayojanam  || 3,14.467 ||
tṛtīyāntaṃ kriyetyetadvigrahe na prayujyate  |
yathā daṇḍaḥ praharaṇaṃ krīḍāyāmiti dṛśyate  || 3,14.468 ||
ghavidhau yacca saṃjñāyāmiti sūtra udāhṛtam  |
upādānaṃ prayogeṣu tasyātyantaṃ na vidyate  || 3,14.469 ||
yairaprayuktaiḥ saṃskāraḥ pradhāneṣu pratīyate  |
te bhede'pi vibhaktīnāṃ nirdiśyanta upādhayaḥ  || 3,14.470 ||
samudāyeṣu vartante bhāvānāṃ sahacāriṇām  |
śabdāstattvavivakṣāyāṃ samuccayavikalpayoḥ  || 3,14.471 ||
samuccayastu kriyate yeṣu pratyarthavṛttiṣu  |
bhedādhiṣṭhānayā yogastesāṃ bhavati saṃkhyayā  || 3,14.472 ||
sarvairviśiṣṭāstairarthairjanyante sahacāribhiḥ  |
buddhayaḥ pratipattṝṇāṃ śabdārthāṃstānato viduḥ  || 3,14.473 ||
saṃsṛṣṭāḥ pratyayeṣvarthāḥ sarva evopakārinaḥ  |
teṣāṃ pratyayarūpeṇa sarveṣāṃ śabdavācyatā  || 3,14.474 ||
kevalānāṃ tu bhāvānāṃ na rūpamavadhāryate  |
anirūpitarūpeṣu teṣu śabdo na vartate  || 3,14.475 ||
pūrvaśabdaprayogācca samūhānna nivartate  |
vartate'vayave nāpi nopāttaṃ tyajate kva cit || 3,14.476 ||
samudāyābhidhāyi ca yadi bhedaṃ viśeṣayet |
tatrātulyavibhaktitvaṃ pūrvakāyādivadbhavet || 3,14.477 ||
samūhe ca pradeśe ca pañcālā iti dṛśyate  |
tathā viśeṣaṇaṃ sarva ityetadupapadyate  || 3,14.478 ||
tathārdhapippalītyatra jātyantaranivṛttaye  |
ardhaṃ ca pippalī ceti khande śabdaḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.479 ||
pañcālānāṃ pradeśo'pi bhinno janapadāntarāt |
tatrānyasya nivṛttyarthe śabde bhedo na gamyate  || 3,14.480 ||
prasiddhāstu viśeṣeṇa samudāye vyavasthitāḥ  |
pradeśe darśanaṃ teṣāmarthaprakaraṇādibhiḥ  || 3,14.481 ||
yadupavyañjanaṃ jāteḥ sahacāri ca karmasu  |
tatra vā rūḍhasaṃbandhaṃ yatprāyeṇopalakṣitam  || 3,14.482 ||
samudāyaḥ pradeśo vetyevaṃ tasminnanāśrite  |
arthātmanyaviśeṣeṇa vartante brāhmaṇādayaḥ  || 3,14.483 ||
yaśca tulyaśrutirdṛṣṭaḥ samudāye vyavasthitaḥ  |
tenopacaritaikatvaṃ pradeśe'pyupalabhyate  || 3,14.484 ||
saṃskārādupaghātādvā vṛtto'ktaparimāṇake  |
tailādau jātiśabdo'tra sāmarthyādavasīyate  || 3,14.485 ||
na jātiguṇaśabdeṣu mūrtibhedo vivakṣitaḥ  |
te jātiguṇasaṃbandha- bhedamātranibandhanāḥ  || 3,14.486 ||
kṛṣṇādivyapadeśaśca sarvāvayavavṛttibhiḥ  |
guṇaiste'pyekadeśasthāḥ paṭādīnāṃ viśeṣakāḥ  || 3,14.487 ||
paṭāvayavavṛttāstu yadā tatra paṭādayaḥ  |
tadā tailādivatteṣāṃ jātiśabdatvamucyate  || 3,14.488 ||
nivṛttyarthā śrutiryeṣāṃ bhedasteṣvanapekṣitaḥ  |
pradeśe samudāye vā guṇo'nyeṣāṃ nivartakaḥ  || 3,14.489 ||
brāhmaṇādhyayane tatra vartate brāhmaṇaśrutiḥ  |
sādṛśyaṃ tatra dṛṣṭaṃ hi kṣatriyādhyayanādibhiḥ  || 3,14.490 ||
brāhmaṇādhyayane vṛttiryadi syādbrāhmaṇaśruteḥ  |
vaktavyaṃ kena dharmeṇa tulyatvaṃ kriyayoriti  || 3,14.491 ||
adhyetari yadā vṛttirucyate brāhmaṇaśruteḥ  |
nimittatvaṃ tadopaiti kriyaivādhyetari sthitā  || 3,14.492 ||
simhaśabdena saṃbandhe gauryamātrābhidhāyinā  |
caitrātṣaṣṭhī prasajyeta yoge śattryādibhiryathā  || 3,14.493 ||
brāhmaṇāyeva dātavyaṃ vaiśyāyetyevamādiṣu  |
saṃpradānādiyogaśca kriyāmātre na kalpate  || 3,14.494 ||
kriyāmātrābhidhāyitvādavyayeṣu vaterna ca  |
pāṭhaḥ kadā citkartavyastulyau pakṣāvubhau yataḥ  || 3,14.495 ||
jahāti jātiṃ dravyaṃ vā tasmānnāvayave sthitaḥ  |
kriyāyāstu śrutiryasmāttadvatyarthe'vatiṣṭhate  || 3,14.496 ||
akriyāṇāṃ nivṛttyarthā, yataścātra kriyāśrutiḥ  |
kriyopalakṣite tasmātkriyāśabdaḥ pratīyate  || 3,14.497 ||
hotavyādiṣu yasmācca kriyānyā brāhmaṇādivat |
apekṣaṇīyā śuddhe'rthe tasmādvṛttirna kasya cit || 3,14.498 ||
sarvaṃ vāpyekadeśo vā yasminnāśriyate kva cit |
viśeṣavṛttiṃ taṃ sarvamāhurbhede vyavasthitam  || 3,14.499 ||
samuccayo vikalpo vā prakārāḥ sarva eva vā  |
viśeṣā iti varṇyante sāmānyaṃ vāvikalpitam  || 3,14.500 ||
na hi brāhmaṇa ityatra bhedaḥ kaścidapāśritaḥ  |
apākṛto vā tenāyaṃ samudāye vyavasthitaḥ  || 3,14.501 ||
kriyā tvāśrīyate yasmin sa bhedo'dhyavasīyate  |
tathānyathā sarvathā cetyaprayoge na vidyate  || 3,14.502 ||
upamāne kriyāvṛttimupameye kriyāśrutiḥ  |
pratyāyayantī bhedasya karotīva padārthatām  || 3,14.503 ||
vyāpāreṇaiva sādṛśye vyāpārasya vivakṣite  |
kriyāvadvacanācchabdātpratyayaḥ pratipādyate  || 3,14.504 ||
kriyāvato'pi sādṛśye vaktumiṣṭe kriyāvatā  |
adhyetā brāhmaṇa iva pratyayo na nivartate  || 3,14.505 ||
adhīte tulya ityevaṃ puṃlliṅgena viśeṣaṇam  |
kriyāvati kriyāyāṃ tu tulyaśabde napuṃsakam  || 3,14.506 ||
prakṛtyarthe viśiṣṭe'pi pratyayārthāviśeṣaṇāt |
putreṇa tulyaḥ kapila iti vṛttiḥ prasajyate  || 3,14.507 ||
yāḥ putre rūḍhasaṃbandhāḥ kriyā loke vivakṣitāḥ  |
tābhiḥ kriyāvataḥ putrādguṇatulye vatirbhavet || 3,14.508 ||
antarbhūtaṃ nimittaṃ ca rūḍhiśabdeṣu yadyapi  |
kriyāstu sahacāriṇyo rūḍhāḥ santi padārthavat || 3,14.509 ||
kramaṃ tu yadi bādhitvā pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇam  |
pradhānānugrahātsāmyādvibhakteścāvatiṣṭhate  || 3,14.510 ||
prakṛteraviśiṣṭatvātkriyātulye prasajyate  |
putrādau guṇaśabdebhyaḥ pūrvoktasya viparyaye  || 3,14.511 ||
sthūlena tulyo yātīti bahiraṅgā kriyāśrutiḥ  |
animittaṃ vatestulyaṃ yātītyatreṣyate vatiḥ  || 3,14.512 ||
dvayaṃ viśeṣyate tena yadekatra viśeṣaṇaṃ  |
tulyaśabdo hi taṃ dharmamubhayasthamapekṣate  || 3,14.513 ||
ekaḥ samāno dharmaścedupamānopameyayoḥ  |
tulayā saṃmitaṃ tulyamiti tatropapadyate  || 3,14.514 ||
sūtre śrutaśca dviṣṭho'sāvabhedena pratīyate  |
na ca sāmānyaśabdatvādaśrutā gamyate kriyā  || 3,14.515 ||
aśrutāśca pratīyante nideśasthāyitādayaḥ  |
ye dharmā niyatāsteṣāṃ putrādiṣu na vidyate  || 3,14.516 ||
anāśritakriyastasmānna tulyo'sti kriyāvatā  |
kriyāyāḥ śravaṇe sāpi kriyāvattā pratīyate  || 3,14.517 ||
dvayoḥ pratividhānācca jyāyastvamabhidhīyate  |
nityāsattvābhidhāyitvātpratyayārthaviśeṣaṇe  || 3,14.518 ||
asattvabhūto vyāpāraḥ kevalaḥ pratyaye yataḥ  |
vidyate lakṣaṇārthatvaṃ nāsti tena kriyāśruteḥ  || 3,14.519 ||
kriyāvatastu grahaṇātprakṛtyarthaviśeṣaṇe  |
kriyāmātrena tulyatve siddhāsattvābhidhāyitā  || 3,14.520 ||
yadā kriyānimittaṃ tu sādṛśyaṃ syātkriyāvatoḥ  |
kriyāvato'bhidheyatvāttadā dravyābhidhāyitā  || 3,14.521 ||
avyayeṣu vateḥ pāṭhaḥ kāryastatra svarādivat |
brāhmaṇena samo'dhyetetyatra ca pratyayo bhavet || 3,14.522 ||
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca vatyarthenāpadiśyate  |
tulyamityanyathā kalpyo vākyaśeṣo'śruto bhavet || 3,14.523 ||
kriyāvatośca sādṛśye pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇe  |
adhyetrā sadṛśo'dhyetetyatra nāsti vatervidhiḥ  || 3,14.524 ||
tulyārthairiti yā tasyāstṛtīyāyā na bhidyate  |
artho bhede'pi sarvābhiritarābhirvibhaktibhiḥ  || 3,14.525 ||
bhojyate brāhmaṇa iva tulyaṃ bhuktaṃ dvijātinā  |
paśyati brāhmaṇamiva tulyaṃ vipreṇa paśyati  || 3,14.526 ||
brāhmaṇeneva vijñātaṃ tulyaṃ jñātaṃ dvijātinā  |
dīyatāṃ brāhmaṇāyeva tulyaṃ vipreṇa dīyatām  || 3,14.527 ||
brāhmaṇādiva vaiśyāttvamadhīṣvādhyayanaṃ bahu  |
ityevamādibhirbhedastṛtīyāyā na kaścana  || 3,14.528 ||
tulyaṃ madhurayādhīye mātrā tulyaṃ smarāmi tām  |
madhurāyāśca mātuśca kathaṃ sādṛśyakalpanā  || 3,14.529 ||
madhurāviṣayaḥ pāṭhaḥ smaraṇaṃ mātṛkarmakam  |
madhurāmātṛśabdābhyāmabhedenābhidhīyate  || 3,14.530 ||
uṣṭrāvayavatulyeṣu mukheṣūṣṭraśrutiryathā  |
vartate gṛhatulye ca prāsāde madhurāśrutiḥ  || 3,14.531 ||
yathādhyayanayoḥ sāmyamadhyetrorapadiśyate  |
tathā kriyāgatairdharmairucyante sādhanāśrayāḥ  || 3,14.532 ||
ivārthe yacca vacanaṃ pūrvasūtre ca yo vidhiḥ  |
kriyāśabdaśrutau bhedo na kaścidvidyate tayoḥ  || 3,14.533 ||
yadyapyupādhiranyatra niyato na prayujyate  |
rūpābhedāttvanirjñātā kriyātra śrūyate punaḥ  || 3,14.534 ||
yathā vyutparayaḥ pucchau kyaṅante sudurādayaḥ  |
satyapi pratyayārthatve bhedābhāvādudāhṛtāḥ  || 3,14.535 ||
evaṃ ca sati pūrveṇa siddho'trāpi vatervidhiḥ  |
niyame vābhidhāne vā bhidyate na kriyāśrutiḥ  || 3,14.536 ||
ive dravyādiviṣayaḥ pratyayaḥ punarucyate  |
kriyāṇāmeva sadṛśve pūrvasūtre vidhīyate  || 3,14.537 ||
madhurāyāmiva gṛhā brāhmaṇasyeva pāṇḍurāḥ  |
ityatra dravyaguṇayoḥ pūrveṇa na vatirbhavet || 3,14.538 ||
ārambhasyākriyārthatve nārtho yogena vidyate  |
ṛte kriyāyā grahaṇātpūrvayogena sidhyati  || 3,14.539 ||
madhurāvayave vṛttirvvākhyātā madhurāśruteḥ  |
brāhmaṇāvayavān dantān vakṣyati brāhmaṇaśrutiḥ  || 3,14.540 ||
na kā cidivayoge tu bāhyātsaṃbandhino  |
ṣaṣṭhī vidhīyate tatra pūrveṇa pratyayo bhavet || 3,14.541 ||
ādhikyaṃ tulyaśabdena saṃbandha upajāyate  |
ṣaṣṭhītṛtīye tatra stastulyaśabdo hi vācakaḥ  || 3,14.542 ||
ivaśabdaprayoge tu bāhyātsaṃbandhino vinā  |
nādhikyamupamāne'sti dyotakaḥ sa prayujyate  || 3,14.543 ||
ive yo vyatireko'tra sa prāsādādihetukaḥ  |
tulye tadviṣayāpekṣamādhikyamupajāyate  || 3,14.544 ||
gavayena samo'nadvāniti vṛttistathā bhavet |
na tvasti gaurivetyatra vyatireka ivāśrayaḥ  || 3,14.545 ||
upameyena saṃbandhātprākprāsādādihetuke  |
vyatireke vaterbhāvo na tulyārthatvahetuke  || 3,14.546 ||
ivaśabdena saṃbandhe na tṛtīyā vidhīyate  |
prakṛtāṃ tāmatastyaktvā vibhaktyantaramāśritam  || 3,14.547 ||
saptamyapi na tatrāsti jñāpakārthā tu sā kṛtā  |
iṣṭā sā śeṣaviṣaye niyatāsu vibhaktiṣu  || 3,14.548 ||
yadi tu vyatirekeṇa viṣaye'smin vibhaktayaḥ  |
pravarteraṃstṛtīyaiva vyabhicāraṃ pradarśayet || 3,14.549 ||
vyabhicāre tathā siddhe saptamīgrahaṇādvinā  |
saptamyevocyate sarvā na santyanyā vibhaktayaḥ  || 3,14.550 ||
atyantamatra viṣaye saptamyā jñāpakārthayā  |
bādhitā vinivarteta ṣaṣṭhī sā gṛhyate punaḥ  || 3,14.551 ||
pūrvābhyāmeva yogābhyāṃ vigrahāntarakalpanāt |
arhārthe'pi vatiḥ siddhaḥ sa tvekena nidarśyate  || 3,14.552 ||
tena tulyamiti prāpte kriyopādhiḥ prasidhyati  |
rājavadvartate rājetyatra bhede vivakṣite  || 3,14.553 ||
rājatvena prasiddhā ye pṛthuprabhṛtayo nṛpāḥ  |
yudhiṣṭhirāntāste'nyeṣāmupamānaṃ mahīkṣitām  || 3,14.554 ||
siddhyasiddhikṛto bheda upamānopameyayoḥ  |
sarvatraiva yato'siddhaṃ prasiddhenopamīyate  || 3,14.555 ||
rājavadrūpamasyeti rājanyeva vivakṣite  |
akriyārthena yogena dvitīyena bhaviṣyati  || 3,14.556 ||
upamānāvivakṣāyāṃ niyamārtho'yamucyate  |
dharmo'rhatikriyākartā tadarthaṃ vacanaṃ punaḥ  || 3,14.557 ||
kṛtahastavadityetatprasiddheṣveva dṛśyate  |
rājatvena prasiddhe ca rājñi rājavadityapi  || 3,14.558 ||
arājñi yeṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ dṛṣṭo'tyantamasaṃbhavaḥ  |
te rājani niyamyante tyajyante vyabhicāriṇaḥ  || 3,14.559 ||
arhateśca kriyā kartrī yā tasyāṃ vatiriṣyate  |
rājānamarhati cchattramiti na tvevamādiṣu  || 3,14.560 ||
prayuktānāṃ hi śabdānāṃ śāstreṇānugamaḥ satām  |
chattrādyarthe tu vacane pratyākhyānaṃ na saṃbhavet || 3,14.561 ||
tadarhamiti nārabdhaṃ sūtraṃ vyākaraṇāntare  |
saṃbhavatyupamātrāpi bhedasya parikalpanāt || 3,14.562 ||
ekasya kāryanirjñānātsiddhasya viṣayāntare  |
taddharmatvavivakṣāyāṃ buddhyā bhedaḥ prakalpyate  || 3,14.563 ||
sūtrārambhānna caitasmādivaśabdasya vidyate  |
prayogaḥ so'pi caitasya viṣaye vidyate vateḥ  || 3,14.564 ||
dasyuhendra ivetyetadaindramantre prayujyate  |
anyatra dṛṣṭakarmendro yathetyasmin vivakṣite  || 3,14.565 ||
pūrvāmavasthāmāśritya yāvasthā vyapadiśyate  |
sadṛśastvaṃ tavaiveti tatraivamabhidhīyate  || 3,14.566 ||
prasiddhabhedaṃ yatrānyadupamānaṃ na vidyate  |
upameyasya tatrātmā svabuddhyā pravibhajyate  || 3,14.567 ||
yo'pi svābhāviko bhedaḥ so'pi buddhinibandhanaḥ  |
tenāsmin viṣaye bhinnamabhinnaṃ vā na vidyate  || 3,14.568 ||
aṅgadī kuṇḍalī ceti darśayan bhedahetubhiḥ  |
caitramīdṛśamityāha buddhyavasthāparigrahāt || 3,14.569 ||
etaiḥ śabdairyathābhūtaḥ pratyayātmopajāyate  |
tatpratyayānukāreṇa viṣayo'pyupapadyate  || 3,14.570 ||
buddhyavasthāvibhāgena bhedakāryaṃ pratīyate  |
janyanta iva śabdānāmarthāḥ sarve vivakṣayā  || 3,14.571 ||
tathāvidhe'pi bāhye'rthe bhidyante yatra buddhayaḥ  |
na tatra kaścitsādṛśyaṃ sadapi pratipadyate  || 3,14.572 ||
atyantaṃ viṣaye bhinne yāvatprakhyā na bhidyate  |
na tāvatpratyabhijñānaṃ kasya cidvinivartate  || 3,14.573 ||
ayameva tu sūtreṇa bhedo bhedena darśitaḥ  |
prasiddhamapi durjñānamabudhaḥ pratipadyate  || 3,14.574 ||
vaiyākaraṇavadbrūte na vaiyākaraṇaḥ sadā  |
vaiyākaraṇavadbrūṣvetyataḥ so'pyabhidhīyate  || 3,14.575 ||
ke citpumāṃso bhāṣante strīvatpuṃvacca yoṣitaḥ  |
vyabhicāre svadharmo'pi punastenopadiśyate  || 3,14.576 ||
sadṛśastvaṃ tavaiveti loke yadabhidhīyate  |
upamānāntaraṃ tatra prasaktaṃ vinivartate  || 3,14.577 ||
yuktamaupayikaṃ rājña ityarthasya nidarśane  |
upamānāvivakṣāyāṃ tadarhamiti paṭhyate  || 3,14.578 ||
prasaktānuprasaktastu vatiśeṣo'bhidhīyate  |
upamānābhisaṃbandhādasmin vatirudāhṛtaḥ  || 3,14.579 ||
pradhānakalpanābhāve guṇaśabdasya darśanāt |
upasargādvatau siddhā dhātau dhātvarthakalpanā  || 3,14.580 ||
svaṃ rūpamiti caitasminnarthasyāpi parigrahaḥ  |
rūpavajjñāpitastasmādāsanno'rtho grahīṣyate  || 3,14.581 ||
dhātvarthenopajanitaṃ sādhanatvena sādhanam  |
dhātunā kṛtamityevamasmin sūtre pratīyate  || 3,14.582 ||
yaḥ śabdaścaritārthatvādatyantaṃ na prayujyate  |
viṣaye'darśanāttatra lopastasyābhidhīyate  || 3,14.583 ||
kriyāyāṃ sādhane dravye prādayo ye vyavasthitāḥ  |
tebhyaḥ sattvābhidhāyibhyo vatiḥ svārthe vidhīyate  || 3,14.584 ||
pratyayena vinā prādistatrārthe na prayujyate  |
bhedena tu samākhyāne vibhāgaḥ parikalpitaḥ  || 3,14.585 ||
anaṅgīkṛtasattvaṃ tu yadi gṛhyeta sādhanam  |
vibhaktibhirniyogaḥ syādyathaiva tasilādiṣu  || 3,14.586 ||
pāṭhādyairavibhaktitvaṃ vatyanteṣvanugamyate  |
teṣāmudvata ityatra vaktavyā savibhaktitā  || 3,14.587 ||
vatyarthaṃ nāvagāhete puṃvadityasya darśanāt |
nañsnañāvapavādasya bādhakaṃ tannipātanam  || 3,14.588 ||
etamutkrāmato nūnaṃ vatyarthaṃ nañsnañāviti  |
tayoḥ pravṛttāvutsargo bādhanānnopapadyate  || 3,14.589 ||
nañsnañau vihitau yena sa yogo nāvagāhate  |
vatiprakaraṇaṃ taddhi liṅgamevaṃ samarthyate  || 3,14.590 ||
abhedenopamānasya bhinnārthopanipātitā  |
ūhastathopamānānāmaṅgavannopalabhyate  || 3,14.591 ||
gāvedhuke carau dṛṣṭā govikartākṣavāpayoḥ  |
paśū rudra iva hyetāvityekavacanaśrutiḥ  || 3,14.592 ||
upamānasya bhedācca bahuṣu syādaño vidhiḥ  |
kāśyapā iti lopaḥ syāttathā pratikṛtiṣvapi  || 3,14.593 ||
evaṃ tu yuktavadbhāvādatraikavacanaṃ bhavet |
luṃ manuṣye tathoktaṃ syālliṅgasyaikasya siddhaye  || 3,14.594 ||
upameyeṣu bhinneṣu kiṃ cidekaṃ pravartate  |
pratyayasya vidhau tatra nityaṃ yuktavadiṣyate  || 3,14.595 ||
yadā pratyupameyaṃ tu tadekaikamavasthitam  |
tadā bāhyārthabhedena taddhitāntaṃ pracīyate  || 3,14.596 ||
yathā samūhapracaye dvigūnāṃ bhinnasaṃkhyatā  |
pañcapūlyādiṣu tathā lubantapracayo bhavet || 3,14.597 ||
pracaye bhidyamāne tu saṃkhyā pūleṣu bhidyate  |
arthabhedo lubanteṣu naivaṃ kaścana dṛśyate  || 3,14.598 ||
yeṣūpameyavacanaḥ śabdo'nyo na prayujyate  |
upamānasya tatrānyaiḥ saṃkhyāyā bheda iṣyate  || 3,14.599 ||
yathā guḍatilādīnāṃ prayogādekasaṃkhyatā  |
pākāderaprayoge tu bhinnā saṃkhyābhidhīyate  || 3,14.600 ||
yaḥ saṃbandhigato bhedaḥ sa prayoge pratīyate  |
saṃbandhināmato bheda upameye na gamyate  || 3,14.601 ||
tasmātsāmānyaśabdatva- prasaṅgavinivṛttaye  |
upameyagato bheda upamāneṣu dṛśyate  || 3,14.602 ||
upamānaṃ samastānāmabhinnaṃ śrūyate kva cit |
bhinnānāmupameyanāmekaikaṃ vopamīyate  || 3,14.603 ||
yathā garuḍa ityetadvyūhāpekṣaṃ prayujyate  |
ekena yatra sādṛśyaṃ vainateyena hastinām  || 3,14.604 ||
ekasyāpi pratīyeta bhinnā pratikṛtiḥ saha  |
kāśyapasyeti tenāyaṃ pratyekamavatiṣṭhate  || 3,14.605 ||
meghāḥ śaila ivetyukte samastānāṃ pratīyate  |
sādṛśyaṃ giriṇaikena pratyekaṃ tena bhidyate  || 3,14.606 ||
chāpekṣā tadviṣayatā vidheyatvānna gamyate  |
kākatālīyamityatra prasiddhaṃ hyupalakṣaṇam  || 3,14.607 ||
rājāśvādiśca viṣayaḥ syādanyo vetyaniścitam  |
tena cchasya vidhānātprāgvyapadeśo na vidyate  || 3,14.608 ||
dvayorivārthayoratra nimittatvaṃ pratīyate  |
ekenāvayavo yuktaḥ pratyayo'nyena yujyate  || 3,14.609 ||
caitrasya tatrāgamanaṃ kākasyāgamanaṃ yathā  |
dasyorabhinipātastu tālasya patanaṃ yathā  || 3,14.610 ||
saṃnipāte tayoryānyā kriyā tatropajāyate  |
vadhādirupameye'rthe tayā chavidhiriṣyate  || 3,14.611 ||
kriyāyāṃ samavetāyāṃ dravyaśabdo'vatiṣṭhate  |
pātāgamanayoḥ kāka- tālaśabdau tathā sthitau  || 3,14.612 ||
yadanvākhyāyakaṃ vākyaṃ tadevaṃ parikalpyate  |
prayogavākvaṃ yalloke tadevaṃ na prayujyate  || 3,14.613 ||
yayoratarkitā prāptirdṛśyate kākatālavat |
tayoḥ samāsaprakṛtervṛttirabhyupagamyate  || 3,14.614 ||
kākasya tālena yathā vadho yasya tu dasyunā  |
tatra citrīkṛte'nyasminnupameye cha iṣyate  || 3,14.615 ||
cañcatprakāraścañcatko bṛhatka iti cāpare  |
maṇimaḍḍūkakhadyotān sādṛśvena pracakṣate  || 3,14.616 ||
tatronmeṣanimeṣābhyāṃ khadyota upamīyate  |
śvāsaprabandhairmaṇḍūkaḥ spandamānaprabho maṇiḥ  || 3,14.617 ||
pravikāsiprabho'lpo'pi mahān ya upalabhyate  |
bṛhatka iti tatraiṣa maṇau śabdaḥ prayujyate  || 3,14.618 ||
sādṛśyameva sarvatra prakāraḥ kaiścidiṣyate  |
bhede'pi tu prakārākhyā kaiścidabhyupagamyate  || 3,14.619 ||
prakāravacanaḥ kaścitprakāravati saṃsthitaḥ  |
prakāramātre vartitvā kaścittadvati vartate  || 3,14.620 ||
sādṛśyagrahaṇaṃ sūtre sadṛśasyopalakṣaṇam  |
tulyayoravyayībhāve sahaśabdo'bhidhāyakaḥ  || 3,14.621 ||
vipsāsādṛśyayorvṛttiryā yathārthābhidhāyinaḥ  |
sa cāyamavyayībhāve bhedo bhedena darśitaḥ  || 3,14.622 ||
sādṛśyaṃ yogyatā kaiścidanāvabhyupagamyate  |
yattu mūrtigataṃ sāmyaṃ tatsahenābhidhīyate  || 3,14.623 ||
itthaṃbhāve'pi sādṛśyaṃ buddhyavasthānibandhanam  |
grahaṇe bhedamātrasya tatrānyaivābhidhīyate  || 3,14.624 ||
gaurvāhīka iti dvitve sādṛśyaṃ pratyudāhṛtam  |
śuklādau sati niṣpanne vāhīko na dvirucyate  || 3,14.625 ||

iti bhartṛharikṛtaṃ vākyapadīyaṃ samāptam

